Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the divisors framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 372. The resulting operator achieves bound O(294/n^5).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the divisors space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 372 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 294. QED.

Theorem 221.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)

Statement: Under the homomorphism theorem assumption on divisors structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 910. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 366 with relations in degree 4.

For the inductive step, we decompose the divisors structure using the homomorphism theorem theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 366. Each subproblem has a solution by the homomorphism theorem fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
